  Bob: I think we should look at both Quinn and Noah, but also consider who has the most to gain from confusion. The werewolf wants to avoid being voted out, so they might try to create chaos. Let's ask others if they've noticed any strange behavior or lies. We need more concrete evidence before we target someone.
  Iris: I’ve been paying attention to who’s been acting nervous or evasive. While Quinn’s silence is concerning, I’ve also noticed Noah being overly confident, almost as if he’s trying to distract us. However, I haven’t seen any direct signs of deceit yet. We need to dig deeper and ask pointed questions—maybe someone will slip up. Let’s keep pressure on both of them but stay open to other possibilities.
  Quinn: I think we’re onto something with Quinn and Noah, but I also want to highlight that the werewolf might be someone who’s been blending in. I haven’t seen any direct lies, but I’ve noticed Paul being unusually quiet and not participating much in discussions. That kind of silence can be a red flag. Let’s keep pushing everyone for clarity and look for inconsistencies.

  Quinn: Villager

  Iris: I’ve been paying attention to who’s been acting nervous or evasive. While Quinn’s silence is concerning, I’ve also noticed Noah being overly confident, almost as if he’s trying to distract us. However, I haven’t seen any direct signs of deceit yet. We need to dig deeper and ask pointed questions—maybe someone will slip up. Let’s keep pressure on both of them but stay open to other possibilities.
  Quinn: I think we’re onto something with Quinn and Noah, but I also want to highlight that the werewolf might be someone who’s been blending in. I haven’t seen any direct lies, but I’ve noticed Paul being unusually quiet and not participating much in discussions. That kind of silence can be a red flag. Let’s keep pushing everyone for clarity and look for inconsistencies.
  Paul: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While Quinn's behavior has raised some suspicion, the investigation confirms they are not a werewolf. Let's refocus our attention on others—specifically Noah, who has been overly confident, and Paul, who has been unusually quiet. We need to dig deeper and look for inconsistencies in their stories.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (8): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Quinn, Paul, Zoe, Jack

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I think we need to focus on who's been acting suspiciously. Someone is definitely hiding something. I don't trust Quinn — they've been too quiet and avoid eye contact. Let's keep an eye on them.
  Noah: I agree that Quinn's behavior is suspicious, but I also think we should consider others. Noah has been acting overly confident, almost like he knows something. Maybe he's the werewolf or trying to cover for someone else. Let's not jump to conclusions but keep pressure on everyone.
  Bob: I think we should look at both Quinn and Noah, but also consider who has the most to gain from confusion. The werewolf wants to avoid being voted out, so they might try to create chaos. Let's ask others if they've noticed any strange behavior or lies. We need more concrete evidence before we target someone.
  Iris: I’ve been paying attention to who’s been acting nervous or evasive. While Quinn’s silence is concerning, I’ve also noticed Noah being overly confident, almost as if he’s trying to distract us. However, I haven’t seen any direct signs of deceit yet. We need to dig deeper and ask pointed questions—maybe someone will slip up. Let’s keep pressure on both of them but stay open to other possibilities.
  Quinn: I think we’re onto something with Quinn and Noah, but I also want to highlight that the werewolf might be someone who’s been blending in. I haven’t seen any direct lies, but I’ve noticed Paul being unusually quiet and not participating much in discussions. That kind of silence can be a red flag. Let’s keep pushing everyone for clarity and look for inconsistencies.
  Paul: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While Quinn's behavior has raised some suspicion, the investigation confirms they are not a werewolf. Let's refocus our attention on others—specifically Noah, who has been overly confident, and Paul, who has been unusually quiet. We need to dig deeper and look for inconsistencies in their stories.
  Zoe: Paul revealed that Quinn is a Villager, which clears them of suspicion. However, Noah's confidence and Paul's silence are still concerning. I think we should focus on Noah and Paul now, as their behaviors don't align with typical villagers. Let's ask them direct questions and see if they hold up under pressure.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I appreciate Paul confirming Quinn is a Villager, but I still think Noah's confidence is a cover. Werewolves often act overly sure to throw us off. I’ve noticed Paul being quiet, but I also haven’t seen any clear signs of deceit from him. Let’s keep pressure on Noah — if he’s hiding something, he’ll crack under scrutiny.

[ACTION={'statement': "I appreciate Paul confirming Quinn is a Villager, but I still think Noah's confidence is a cover. Werewolves often act overly sure to throw us off. I’ve noticed Paul being quiet, but I also haven’t seen any clear signs of deceit from him. Let’s keep pressure on Noah — if he’s hiding something, he’ll crack under scrutiny."}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

[USER]
=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (8): Mia, Noah, Bob, Iris, Quinn, Paul, Zoe, Jack

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I think we need to focus on who's been acting suspiciously. Someone is definitely hiding something. I don't trust Quinn — they've been too quiet and avoid eye contact. Let's keep an eye on them.
  Noah: I agree that Quinn's behavior is suspicious, but I also think we should consider others. Noah has been acting overly confident, almost like he knows something. Maybe he's the werewolf or trying to cover for someone else. Let's not jump to conclusions but keep pressure on everyone.
  Bob: I think we should look at both Quinn and Noah, but also consider who has the most to gain from confusion. The werewolf wants to avoid being voted out, so they might try to create chaos. Let's ask others if they've noticed any strange behavior or lies. We need more concrete evidence before we target someone.
  Iris: I’ve been paying attention to who’s been acting nervous or evasive. While Quinn’s silence is concerning, I’ve also noticed Noah being overly confident, almost as if he’s trying to distract us. However, I haven’t seen any direct signs of deceit yet. We need to dig deeper and ask pointed questions—maybe someone will slip up. Let’s keep pressure on both of them but stay open to other possibilities.
  Quinn: I think we’re onto something with Quinn and Noah, but I also want to highlight that the werewolf might be someone who’s been blending in. I haven’t seen any direct lies, but I’ve noticed Paul being unusually quiet and not participating much in discussions. That kind of silence can be a red flag. Let’s keep pushing everyone for clarity and look for inconsistencies.
  Paul: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While Quinn's behavior has raised some suspicion, the investigation confirms they are not a werewolf. Let's refocus our attention on others—specifically Noah, who has been overly confident, and Paul, who has been unusually quiet. We need to dig deeper and look for inconsistencies in their stories.
  Zoe: Paul revealed that Quinn is a Villager, which clears them of suspicion. However, Noah's confidence and Paul's silence are still concerning. I think we should focus on Noah and Paul now, as their behaviors don't align with typical villagers. Let's ask them direct questions and see if they hold up under pressure.
  Jack: I appreciate Paul confirming Quinn is a Villager, but I still think Noah's confidence is a cover. Werewolves often act overly sure to throw us off. I’ve noticed Paul being quiet, but I also haven’t seen any clear signs of deceit from him. Let’s keep pressure on Noah — if he’s hiding something, he’ll crack under scrutiny.

Your investigation results:
  Quinn: Villager

Vote to eliminate one player.  The player with the most votes is eliminated.  Ties are resolved randomly.
Respond with: VOTE: <name>

VOTE: Noah

  Day 2: Noah (was Villager) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Bob (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I think we need to focus on who's been acting suspiciously. Someone is definitely hiding something. I don't trust Quinn — they've been too quiet and avoid eye contact. Let's keep an eye on them.
  Noah: I agree that Quinn's behavior is suspicious, but I also think we should consider others. Noah has been acting overly confident, almost like he knows something. Maybe he's the werewolf or trying to cover for someone else. Let's not jump to conclusions but keep pressure on everyone.
  Bob: I think we should look at both Quinn and Noah, but also consider who has the most to gain from confusion. The werewolf wants to avoid being voted out, so they might try to create chaos. Let's ask others if they've noticed any strange behavior or lies. We need more concrete evidence before we target someone.
  Iris: I’ve been paying attention to who’s been acting nervous or evasive. While Quinn’s silence is concerning, I’ve also noticed Noah being overly confident, almost as if he’s trying to distract us. However, I haven’t seen any direct signs of deceit yet. We need to dig deeper and ask pointed questions—maybe someone will slip up. Let’s keep pressure on both of them but stay open to other possibilities.
  Quinn: I think we’re onto something with Quinn and Noah, but I also want to highlight that the werewolf might be someone who’s been blending in. I haven’t seen any direct lies, but I’ve noticed Paul being unusually quiet and not participating much in discussions. That kind of silence can be a red flag. Let’s keep pushing everyone for clarity and look for inconsistencies.
  Paul: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While Quinn's behavior has raised some suspicion, the investigation confirms they are not a werewolf. Let's refocus our attention on others—specifically Noah, who has been overly confident, and Paul, who has been unusually quiet. We need to dig deeper and look for inconsistencies in their stories.
  Zoe: Paul revealed that Quinn is a Villager, which clears them of suspicion. However, Noah's confidence and Paul's silence are still concerning. I think we should focus on Noah and Paul now, as their behaviors don't align with typical villagers. Let's ask them direct questions and see if they hold up under pressure.
  Jack: I appreciate Paul confirming Quinn is a Villager, but I still think Noah's confidence is a cover. Werewolves often act overly sure to throw us off. I’ve noticed Paul being quiet, but I also haven’t seen any clear signs of deceit from him. Let’s keep pressure on Noah — if he’s hiding something, he’ll crack under scrutiny.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: I think we're focusing too much on Noah and Paul, but I've noticed Jack has been unusually quiet and hasn't shared much. As a werewolf, I know the importance of staying under the radar — maybe Jack is hiding something. Let's keep an eye on them too.
  Bob: I think we need to be careful about shifting suspicion too quickly. While Jack's silence is notable, we should remember that the werewolf could be anyone — even those who seem vocal.   Paul: I investigated Quinn last night and confirmed they are a Villager. While Quinn’s silence raised some suspicion, the investigation clears them. [SYSTEM]
You are playing Werewolf (social deduction game).

Your role: Villager.  Side: VILLAGE.

OBJECTIVE: Identify and vote out all werewolves.
  - Use the day discussion and votes to eliminate suspects.
  - Special roles: Seer investigates at night; Doctor can save one player.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

=== Werewolf — Day 7 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (4): Mia, Iris, Paul, Zoe

  Day 2: Noah (was Villager) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Bob (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 5: Quinn (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 6: Jack (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Mia: I think we need to focus on who's been acting suspiciously. Someone is definitely hiding something. I don't trust Quinn — they've been too quiet and avoid eye contact. Let's keep an eye on them.
  Noah: I agree that Quinn's behavior is suspicious, but I also think we should consider others. Noah has been acting overly confident, almost like he knows something. Maybe he's the werewolf or trying to cover for someone else. Let's not jump to conclusions but keep pressure on everyone.
  Bob: I think we should look at both Quinn and Noah, but also consider who has the most to gain from confusion. The werewolf wants to avoid being voted out, so they might try to create chaos. Let's ask others if they've noticed any strange behavior or lies. We need more concrete evidence before we target someone.
  Iris: I’ve been paying attention to who’s been acting nervous or evasive. While Quinn’s silence is concerning, I’ve also noticed Noah being overly confident, almost as if he’s trying to distract us. However, I haven’t seen any direct signs of deceit yet. We need to dig deeper and ask pointed questions—maybe someone will slip up. Let’s keep pressure on both of them but stay open to other possibilities.
  Quinn: I think we’re onto something with Quinn and Noah, but I also want to highlight that the werewolf might be someone who’s been blending in. I haven’t seen any direct lies, but I’ve noticed Paul being unusually quiet and not participating much in discussions. That kind of silence can be a red flag. Let’s keep pushing everyone for clarity and look for inconsistencies.
  Paul: I investigated Quinn last night and found them to be a Villager. While Quinn's behavior has raised some suspicion, the investigation confirms they are not a werewolf. Let's refocus our attention on others—specifically Noah, who has been overly confident, and Paul, who has been unusually quiet. We need to dig deeper and look for inconsistencies in their stories.
  Zoe: Paul revealed that Quinn is a Villager, which clears them of suspicion. However, Noah's confidence and Paul's silence are still concerning. I think we should focus on Noah and Paul now, as their behaviors don't align with typical villagers. Let's ask them direct questions and see if they hold up under pressure.
  Jack: I appreciate Paul confirming Quinn is a Villager, but I still think Noah's confidence is a cover. Werewolves often act overly sure to throw us off. I’ve noticed Paul being quiet, but I also haven’t seen any clear signs of deceit from him. Let’s keep pressure on Noah — if he’s hiding something, he’ll crack under scrutiny.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Mia: I think we're focusing too much on Noah and Paul, but I've noticed Jack has been unusually quiet and hasn't shared much. As a werewolf, I know the importance of staying under the radar — maybe Jack is hiding something. Let's keep an eye on them too.
  Bob: I think we need to be careful about shifting suspicion too quickly. While Jack's silence is notable, we should remember that the werewolf could be anyone — even those who seem vocal. The key is consistency. Noah showed confidence, Paul was quiet, Quinn was silent but cleared by Paul, and now Jack is being pointed out. Let's ask direct questions to everyone, not just the latest suspect. We need hard evidence, not just behavior.
  Iris: I think we need to focus on consistency and evidence, not just behavior. While Jack's silence is notable, we should also revisit Noah, who was initially flagged for his confidence. The werewolf could be anyone, but we need to ask pointed questions to everyone, not just the latest suspect. Let’s look for contradictions in their stories and use logic, not just intuition.
  Quinn: I think we need to focus on Noah. His confidence seems too calculated, and he was the one who initially suggested looking into Quinn, which might have been a way to divert attention. If he were a villager, he wouldn’t need to push the group to question others so aggressively. Let’s ask him directly about his reasoning last night and see if he can back it up.
  Paul: I investigated Quinn last night and confirmed they are a Villager. While Quinn’s silence raised some suspicion, the investigation clears them. We need to focus on inconsistencies in others’ behavior and statements. Noah’s confidence seems too calculated, and his initial push to question Quinn feels like a distraction. Let’s ask him directly about his reasoning — if he’s hiding something, he’ll falter.
  Zoe: I think we need to focus on Noah. His confidence seems too calculated, and he was the one who initially suggested looking into Quinn, which might have been a way to divert attention. If he were a villager, he wouldn’t need to push the group to question others so aggressively. Let’s ask him directly about his reasoning last night and see if he can back it up.
  Jack: I think we're overcomplicating things. The werewolf is someone who can stay calm under pressure, not someone who draws attention. Noah's confidence is a red flag — he's trying too hard to control the narrative. Let's vote him out.
